However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [110] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量和气象遥感）构建预测模型，评估尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区冰川湖溃决、降雨触发滑坡及冰川小洪水三种灾害的易发性和触发时机。基于589个历史溃决事件和数千滑坡记录，通过空间交叉验证发现：前期天气对触发时机预测有效（ROC 0.73-0.83），地形对易发性排序仅部分有效（0.54-0.76），深度模型未显著超越梯度提升基线。最终提供尼泊尔优先预警清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现雷达干涉可识别不稳定的冰碛坝、卫星气象可标记湖泊受压时段，但缺乏将两者结合的预测模型，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用HMAGLOFDB中589个溃决事件及数千滑坡记录，将每个事件与相似的未失稳站点匹配；采用空间交叉验证（按地图瓦片分割）防止空间泄漏；比较梯度提升基线模型与五种深度学习模型；分别对三种灾害建立'易发性排名'和'触发时机'两个独立问题模型。

Result: 前期天气指标在触发时机预测上表现良好（大溃决ROC 0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82）；地形排名的诚实估计为0.76、0.71和0.54（小洪水无异于随机）；溃决信号在尼泊尔单区域内达0.89；深度学习模型未优于简单基线，基线由三规则决策树（粗糙度和季风降雨）支撑。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以支持中等效度的灾害预警排序，但仍有局限；最终产出尼泊尔风险排序清单作为优先化辅助工具而非精确预测，并明确指出免费数据的边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [111] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH架构，通过周期性缓存状态检查点作为状态锚点，并结合内容条件的锚键进行注意力式聚合，在保持高效解码的同时将状态空间模型的记忆能力扩展到固定维度之外。实验表明MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽具备随上下文长度增长的分词级记忆，但训练时计算复杂度为二次方，推理时键值缓存线性增长；而循环替代方案虽解码高效，但因早期关联被后续更新覆盖，在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。现有方法无法同时兼顾长上下文检索能力和计算效率。

Method: MARCH周期性缓存累积的循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，每个锚点关联紧凑的内容条件锚键，形成随上下文长度增长的可控记忆库。在每个token处，MARCH生成锚查询，对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力式聚合，将聚合结果沿当前状态传递。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上一致优于多种线性注意力变体，证明内容路由的状态缓存显著增强了循环长程记忆能力。

Conclusion: MARCH通过内容路由的状态缓存机制，在保留循环网络原生高效计算路径的同时有效扩大了长期记忆容量，为状态空间模型在长序列任务上的性能提升提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [112] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA三层控制架构，用于多AUV自组网环境下的协同目标跟踪，通过扩散策略与值梯度引导结合，解决了高维状态空间、噪声敏感和训练不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网协同跟踪面临水声通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动等挑战，现有MARL方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模困难、策略生成对噪声敏感，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化。

Method: 提出VGG-MADiffRL算法，在扩散策略基础上引入值梯度引导反向去噪过程中的动作生成，使动作朝向更高期望回报；采用双子价值网络联合优化和软目标更新来缓解过估计和训练振荡。同时提出MDCA三层闭环控制架构（全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层、物理动作执行层），实现任务分配、局部决策与执行反馈的协同优化。

Result: 实验表明VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪提供了一种将扩散模型与值引导相结合的新型MARL解决方案，在实际水下动态环境中展现出良好的工程应用前景。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [113] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将生成式建模统一表述为路径积分形式，通过MSRJD形式将流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型纳入统一"主作用量"框架，并利用图论微扰论提出单圈修正方案，显著提升确定性采样器精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式建模领域存在多种看似不同的模型范式（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型），缺乏统一的数学框架来揭示其内在联系与理论基础，阻碍了跨模型方法的理论进步与系统性改进。

Method: 将生成式建模形式化为路径积分，利用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de Dominicis（MSRJD）形式将主作用量分解为自由与相互作用部分，引入图论微扰展开；由此推导出单圈修正项（无需额外随机采样成本），并提出响应加权的分数匹配目标以及基于EFT功率计数的对称等变漂移设计算子展开。

Result: 在可解析求解及非线性漂移的验证中，提出的一圈修正方案将树级误差从53%显著降低至1.6%，展示了该理论框架对实际问题预测能力的显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成式建模提供了统一的路径积分理论框架，不仅统一了现有主流范式，还开辟了系统性的微扰修正路径和非平凡的目标函数改进方向，具有重要的理论价值与应用潜力。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [114] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图异常检测模型AddGraph的严格事后可解释性框架，通过双时空归因（DSTA）机制提供空间、短期时间与长期时间三个维度的解释，在不改变检测性能（Delta AUC = 0）的前提下为原本完全不透明的模型添加可解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的动态图异常检测器虽然准确率高，但完全不透明——当检测到异常边时只给出分数而不给出原因，这在需要可审计性和可信度的合作监管信息系统中是不可接受的。AddGraph作为基础的GCN+GRU框架从未配备任何可解释性机制，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: X-AddGraph基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块分别对齐的组件：(1)基于梯度的邻接结构相关性归因（空间维度）；(2)直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；(3)通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。检测器保持冻结状态，因此检测性能完全不变。

Result: 检测性能精确保持（Delta AUC = 0，实证验证精确到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准上，训练好的AddGraph基线达到平均每快照AUC 0.8705，超过原始发表结果。在四类边样本（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上，长期归因识别出的历史快照携带显著更多的反事实信号（0.127 vs. 0.074），这是空间盲解释器无法提供的。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph成功为AddGraph在不牺牲检测性能的前提下增添了三维可解释性，尤其是长期时间归因具有独特的反事实解释能力，填补了AddGraph在可解释性领域的空白，并开源实现以供完全复现。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [115] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次利用多评分者（multi-scored）睡眠分期数据集，提出一种基于学习的睡眠图（LBH）构建方法，通过混淆矩阵对每位评分者的特定行为建模，聚合多个专家的判断来生成更可靠的参考睡眠分期标签。在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上，使用随机森林+EEG+EMG的组合达到约86%的准确率和F1分数，优于数据集睡眠图（DH）和最佳评分者睡眠图（BSH）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化睡眠分期研究通常只针对单一参考睡眠图进行模型评估，但已知不同评分者之间存在标注变异性（inter-scorer variability）。本文的动机是探究是否可以利用多评分者标注数据，从多位专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标签，从而提高睡眠分期评估的准确性。

Method: 使用公开的DOD-H和DOD-O数据集，将EEG（C3-M2）和下巴EMG信号分割为30秒的epoch，每个模态提取30个特征，共60个特征。提出基于学习的睡眠图（LBH）方法：利用机器学习模型生成每位评分者的混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计每个真实睡眠阶段在给定评分者标签下的概率，再将多评分者的概率聚合以分配最终标签。使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知机三个分类器，在仅EEG和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估，并与数据集睡眠图（DH）和最佳评分者睡眠图（BSH）进行比较。

Result: LBH在所有设定下均一致优于对比方法。最佳结果为随机森林+EEG+EMG组合：在DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；在DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在不丢弃任何单个专家信息的前提下，改进参考睡眠图的构建质量，为多评分者睡眠分期数据的利用提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [116] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型的残差流中存在以"预测方向"（token的unembedding方向）为核心的内容定义特权锚点。所有18个测试模型（稠密和MoE，7B-120B，base和指令微调）中，残差流变化在几何和行为上按与预测方向的接近程度分层：预测近端区域高度结构化、能聚类相关提示，远端区域平坦且抗区分。该组织方式难以被基于方差的分析完全捕获，且预测界面虽窄但在因果上起决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型会发展出特权基（坐标轴统计特性与残差流其余部分不同），但这类基会选择什么样的方向尚不清楚。作者希望理解残差流中这种组织化的几何原理，尤其是高维计算如何与线性读出共存的问题。

Method: 以预测方向（模型当前预测token的unembedding方向）为锚点，测量残差流中所有方向与该锚点的几何接近度，并将残差流变化按距离分层分析。通过对18个模型（稠密和MoE架构，7B-120B规模，基础模型和指令微调模型）进行几何分析、因果干预（扰动不同距离层次的方向）和时间动力学分析来验证该组织的普遍性和功能性。

Result: 所有18个模型中均观察到分层现象。预测方向几乎与主方差轴正交，导致基于方差的分析只能部分恢复该组织，且随提示异质性增加而失效。因果实验显示：扰动最接近预测方向的方差方向会导致立即发散和频繁的任务框架切换；扰动次近方向仅延迟发散且保持框架。远端区域虽逐方向弱对齐读出，但在因果和时间上承担重要负载，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是一种与先前描述坐标轴不同的特权锚点。该发现为高维计算与线性读出共存提供了几何解释：窄而集中的预测接口承载读出相关结构，而广阔的互补区域随模型规模扩展，两者在不同因果层面共同支撑模型行为。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [117] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，用于对飞行器阵风诱导载荷进行归类。该方法通过编码大量实验观测数据，并采用摘要化程序选取最小的高显著性样本集，从而建立基于相似性的客观分类标准。研究基于6种飞行姿态下3480个压力载荷测量数据，识别出9种跨姿态反复出现的基本响应类型，且可通过瞬态响应分析获得底层流体力学物理直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对阵风诱导载荷的分类依赖于飞行姿态等粗参数标注，作者希望找到一种客观的分类准则，既能组织跨多种飞行条件下阵风载荷的复杂性，又能保持与粗参数标注同等的可解释性。

Method: 采用机器学习表示对大量实验观测进行编码，并应用摘要化（summarization）程序选择最小的高显著性示例（exemplars），以这些示例作为基于相似性的客观分类依据。数据集为6种飞行姿态下3480个随机阵风压力载荷测量。

Result: 识别出9种跨多个姿态反复出现的基本响应类型，并对各类型的瞬态响应进行分析，揭示了其背后流体力学机制的物理直觉。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够提供一种客观、可解释且便于专家检验的分类准则，所选示例可作为更精细化实验的素材，有助于深入理解阵风诱导载荷的流体力学本质。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估临床预测模型的框架，将患者分为结果可观察的"确定案例"和因治疗决策导致结果无法观察的"不确定案例"，并分别评估模型在两类案例上的表现，揭示标准评估指标可能掩盖重要的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型通常假设每个患者的结果都能被清晰观察到，但治疗决策可能使临床相关的结局永久不可观察。以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为例，许多患者的结果因治疗决策而变得不确定，传统评估指标难以捕捉这种情况下的模型失败模式。

Method: 使用2,497名心脏骤停患者队列（其中1,429名患者的结局因治疗决策而不确定），由独立临床专家对不确定案例的反事实结局进行猜测。提出将评估明确分为确定案例和不确定案例两部分的框架，并提出一种同时使用两类案例目标标签的简单预测模型，以在两者之间进行权衡。

Result: 在提出的神经模型和多种表格基线模型中，具有相似确定案例AUROC的模型在确定案例Brier分数和对不确定案例的概率估计上可能存在显著差异。提高与不确定案例目标标签的一致性通常以降低确定案例的准确性为代价，这凸显了标准评估所掩盖的明确权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床有意义的结果是否可观察时，传统评估指标可能遗漏最需要预后支持的患者的重大失败模式，因此需要更细致的评估框架来应对这一挑战。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [119] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线策略评估（OPE）中的确定性top-k目标规则进行了系统基准测试，对比了六种估计器在五个数据集和两组已知效应扫描下的表现，并通过非模拟配对参考验证了机制。研究发现三个核心问题：弱重叠受日志器与目标行为对齐程度而非日志锐度主导；优化器诅咒无法通过交叉拟合结果干扰项修复，需要诚实的策略级分割改变估计目标；倾向性估计误差是最大的性能退化来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下决定对谁进行干预，希望在部署目标规则前知道该规则能带来多少收益。离线策略评估（OPE）可以基于日志数据实现这一目标，但可部署的规则是确定性top-k策略，它消除了所有对动作的平均化，导致弱重叠问题直接影响估计结果。现有方法在该场景下的表现缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 对六种估计器进行基准测试，使用五个数据集和两组已知效应扫描；通过非模拟配对参考验证机制；分析弱重叠、优化器诅咒和倾向性估计误差三个关键机制，并将倾向性概率下限设为0.02以保证权重有界。

Result: 弱重叠由日志器与目标行为的对齐程度决定，而非日志锐度；交叉拟合干扰项无法修复优化器诅咒且使其更严重；倾向性估计误差对IPS伤害最大，对双稳健估计几乎无影响，甚至可能逆转重叠诊断本身。倾向性下限使两个调优混合估计器退化为未调优父级，最终只有四个实际不同的估计器。

Conclusion: 有效样本量能对日志环境间的风险排序，但在实践者持有的单个日志内对候选排序能力较弱，且其截断点不可迁移。解决优化器诅咒需要改变估计目标而非去偏全样本策略。基准测试已公开，仅使用公开数据。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [120] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HouseGNN（Householder图神经网络），通过使用Householder反射器和GroupSort激活函数构建分段正交层，解决深度GNN中的过平滑问题，保证每层节点嵌入的欧氏范数不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络面临过平滑问题，即随着网络深度增加，节点表示逐渐坍缩到低信息子空间，导致深层GNN性能退化。

Method: HouseGNN不同于标准GCN直接更新隐藏状态，而是利用聚合的邻居消息估计反射方向，然后通过Householder反射器和GroupSort更新节点嵌入，形成分段正交层，在每一节点和每一深度保持欧氏范数。

Result: 证明了三个核心性质：(i) 每个内部层保持节点级别的欧氏范数；(ii) Householder反射器对消息具有尺度和符号不变性；(iii) 节点间的成对距离可通过节点级正交算子之间的不匹配而发生变化。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过分段正交变换机制从理论上保证范数守恒，有效缓解深层GNN的过平滑问题，同时保持节点间区分度。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [121] [GENADA: efficient generative time series adversarial attack framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12535)
*Michael Baronov,Denis Vorobev,Margarita Rusanova,Petr Sokerin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GENADA，一种基于生成模型的对抗攻击方法，通过单次前向传播直接生成欺骗性扰动，避免了传统梯度迭代攻击中反复反向传播的计算开销，在时间序列领域达到与强基线相当的攻击效果且推理更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列分析中易受对抗攻击，但现有的梯度迭代攻击方法需要多次反向传播计算输入梯度，计算开销巨大，亟需更高效的攻击生成方案。

Method: 提出GENADA框架，学习一个生成模型在单次前向传播中直接产生对抗扰动，并设计了相应的训练流程。同时提供了单步和迭代两种生成攻击变体。

Result: 在多个神经网络模型和时间序列数据集上的低维受控实验中，GENADA实现了与强基线相当的攻击质量，同时在推理阶段生成扰动所需时间更少。

Conclusion: 生成式对抗攻击在保持攻击效果的同时显著提升了推理效率，为时间序列领域的高效对抗攻击提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are widely used for time series analysis in domains such as healthcare, finance, energy systems, and environmental monitoring. However, these models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small input perturbations cause severe degradation in predictive performance. Commonly used gradient-based attacks, iterative first-order methods, are computationally burdensome, as they repeatedly backpropagate through the victim model to compute input gradients during a number of iterative refinement steps. We propose a GENerative ADversarial Attack (GENADA) that learns a generative model to produce deceptive perturbations directly in a single forward pass and a procedure to train it. Variants include single-step and iterative generative attack schemes. The validation considers attacks on several neural models and datasets in the time-series domain, a controlled, low-dimensional setting. Empirically, GENADA achieves comparable attack quality to strong baselines while requiring less time to generate perturbations during inference.

</details>


### [122] [Scaling Automatic Research Agents via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12564)
*Xiyuan Yang,Sheikh Sarwar,Jingru Cheng,Zhan Shi,Duanshun Li,Huiyuan Chen,Haiyang Zhang,Chenlei Guo,Jingrui He,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出World Model RL（WMRL）方法，解决自动科研（AutoResearch）智能体在强化学习训练中环境执行成为瓶颈的问题，通过用世界模型替代真实环境执行实现3-4倍加速，并通过在线去偏和逆方差去噪两种策略应对世界模型的不完美性，在理论和实验上都取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科研智能体的RL训练中，智能体生成和环境执行两个组件扩展方式截然不同：所有生成通过批处理共享计算，而每个执行占用独立的沙箱和真实机器时间，导致环境执行主导训练成本并成为瓶颈。这一根本性张力限制了RL扩展的效率。

Method: 提出WMRL框架，用世界模型替代环境执行以消除瓶颈；针对世界模型的不完美性（奖励包含偏差和噪声），提出两种缓解策略：在线去偏（Online Debiasing）抵消偏差，逆方差去噪（Inverse-Variance Denoising）抑制噪声；提供理论收敛保证和实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明两种缓解策略严格改善收敛保证；实验上WMRL在不同任务和智能体规模上加速训练3-4倍，性能超过标准RL基线；后训练的4B和9B智能体在留出基准上超越48B和120B的大型开源智能体；方法还成功迁移到具身VLA策略的后训练中。

Conclusion: WMRL有效解决了AutoResearch智能体RL训练中环境执行的瓶颈问题，通过世界模型替代和环境模型的偏噪缓解，在保证性能的同时大幅提升训练效率，并且方法具有良好的泛化性，可迁移到其他场景如具身VLA策略训练。

Abstract: Automating empirical research is a long-standing direction of AI. Recent automatic research (AutoResearch) agents bring this goal within reach, as modern LLMs show the capability to independently implement solutions and learn from the execution outcomes. Behind these gains, post-training (especially RL) plays a central role. In this paper, we identify a fundamental tension when scaling RL for these agents: the two components of every AutoResearch trajectory (agent generation and environment execution) scale in very different manners, since all generation shares compute through batching, while each execution occupies its exclusive sandbox and real machine time. As a result, the environment execution dominates the training cost and becomes the bottleneck as trajectories grow. To resolve this tension, we propose World Model RL (WMRL), which replaces environment execution with a world model to remove this bottleneck. Additionally, the world model can be imperfect, as its rewards are corrupted by bias and noise. Therefore, we further equip WMRL with two mitigations, Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, which offset the bias and suppress the noise respectively. Theoretically, we prove that both mitigations of WMRL strictly improve the convergence guarantee. Empirically, WMRL accelerates training by 3-4x on various tasks at different agent scales, while exceeding the performance of standard RL baselines. Moreover, our post-trained 4B and 9B agents outperform much larger open-weight agents of 48B and 120B on held-out benchmarks. Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [123] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种快速、可扩展且与数据分布无关的top-k选择算法，通过单遍过滤（随机采样估计自适应阈值 + 紧凑缓冲区 + 精确top-k例程）实现概率正确性保证，相比PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5x-10x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据规模增长，现有top-k选择方法效率低下：精确方法计算和内存开销高，近似方法依赖脆弱的启发式规则，在对抗性或重尾输入下性能退化，缺乏对输入分布鲁棒性的保证。

Method: Prof-K执行单遍过滤流程：首先用少量随机样本估计自适应阈值，然后将N个输入元素流式写入紧凑缓冲区，最后在缓冲区上运行精确top-k例程。通过高概率界推导正确性和缓冲区大小保证，并给出近似最优样本规模以最小化开销。

Result: Prof-K相比PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5x-10x加速，在大型数据和小到中等k值场景提升最显著。通过放松召回率目标（如恢复95%真实top-k），可提供原理性的精度-速度权衡。在训练BatchTopK稀疏自编码器中也展示了实际影响。

Conclusion: Prof-K提供与输入分布无关的概率正确性保证（至少1-ε概率恢复真实top-k元素），确保对对抗性设置的鲁棒性，同时在大规模场景中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [124] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCoGen，一个两阶段的生理信号条件生成框架。第一阶段为每个模态训练掩码自编码器，将时变信号蒸馏为紧凑且容忍缺失的token序列；第二阶段用流匹配生成器融合这些token与静态条件，合成目标信号。在三个基准上（AI-READI连续血糖监测、MIMIC-III/IV动脉血压生成），ReCoGen在全部16个（数据集、任务、指标）组合中超越六个代表性条件生成器，其中13个达到或超过真实信号的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多高信息量的生理信号（如有创血压）具有侵入性、昂贵或对特定患者不可用。现有条件生成器只围绕单一条件模态设计，在处理实践中异质的、不规则缺失的时变信号与静态协变量混合场景时性能退化，亟需一种能处理多模态异构条件的生成框架。

Method: ReCoGen采用两阶段解耦设计：Stage I对每个模态分别训练掩码自编码器，将时变条件信号蒸馏为紧凑且对缺失鲁棒的token序列；Stage II训练流匹配生成器，通过可学习的交叉注意力融合冻结的逐模态编码器token，并采用"token+自适应层归一化（AdaLN）"双路径融合静态条件，最终合成目标信号。

Result: 在包含AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III/IV动脉血压生成的三个生理基准上，ReCoGen在全部16个（数据集、任务、指标）设置中取得了最佳下游效用，超越六个代表性条件生成器；其中13个设置的效用达到或超过真实信号的效用（视为近似锚点而非上限）。消融实验证明收益来自条件路径的设计。

Conclusion: ReCoGen通过解耦多模态条件表示与目标生成，将常规收集的信号转化为侵入性或不可用信号的有效替代，为更少侵入、更低成本的连续临床监测迈出一步。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [125] [Predicting When Random Low-Dimensional Reparameterizations Train Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12597)
*Andrew Cheng,Ali Eslamian,Jie Cheng,Mehdi Zargham,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机低维重参数化训练神经网络时，潜在搜索空间需要多大才能达到低损失区域的问题。作者提出了一个方向分辨的二次主公式，用于预测随机切片残差，并引入了Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN)框架，通过结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射避免稠密随机映射的存储开销，同时开发了无矩阵曲率逼近和无扫描维度选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可以通过随机低维重参数化进行训练或微调，但一个关键的实际问题是：潜在搜索空间的维度需要多大才能达到低损失区域？现有理论尚不能准确预测这一维度需求。

Method: 1) 将已知的可达性转变表达为等价的锥形式，以极锥的统计维度为中心；2) 提出方向分辨的二次主公式，从曲率谱和参考到解的位移轮廓预测随机切片残差；3) 引入RaMaN框架，使用结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射实例化预测的潜在维度，避免O(dP)的存储，将优化器状态内存从O(P)降到O(d)；4) 开发无矩阵曲率逼近和无扫描维度选择。

Result: 实验表明方向分辨预测器能紧密跟踪受控二次和神经曲率实验中的转变位置，在位移方向重要时优于方向无关的近似。端到端实验在图像和语言模型上展示了尖锐的、协议依赖的训练转变。

Conclusion: 方向分辨的预测框架能够更准确地预测随机低维重参数化的可达性转变，RaMaN实现了内存高效且实用的随机映射网络训练方案。

Abstract: Neural networks can often be trained or fine-tuned through random low-dimensional reparameterization, where a small latent vector is mapped into a full parameter update by a frozen random map. This raises a practical question: how large must the latent search space be to reach a low-loss region? We first express the known accessibility transition in an equivalent conic form, centered for compact convex targets at the statistical dimension of the polar cone. Our main theoretical contribution is an orientation-resolved quadratic master formula that predicts the random-slice residual from both the curvature spectrum and the reference-to-solution displacement profile. It yields a self-consistent isotropic-orientation predictor and, in a conservative radius-only specialization, recovers the earlier Gaussian-width quadratic bound. Building on this analysis, we introduce Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN), which instantiate the predicted latent dimension using structured Hadamard or seed-regenerated Gaussian maps. These constructions avoid the O(dP) storage of dense random maps and reduce optimizer-state memory from O(P) to O(d). We also develop matrix-free curvature approximations and sweep-free dimension selection. Across controlled quadratic and neural-curvature experiments, the orientation-resolved predictor closely tracks measured transition locations and outperforms orientation-agnostic approximations when displacement direction matters. End-to-end experiments further show sharp, protocol-dependent training transitions across image and language models.

</details>


### [126] [The Boolean Power of ReLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12617)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限简单无向图且带有单一布尔节点特征的情况下，对于任意由最终常数激活函数构成的集合Σ，Σ-MPLang可表达的布尔查询严格包含于ReLU-MPLang可表达的布尔查询类中，从而解决了trReLU-MPLang与ReLU-MPLang在布尔查询表达能力上孰强孰弱的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决一个近期提出的开放问题：在布尔查询方面，ReLU-MPLang是否比trReLU-MPLang更具表达能力。这直接关系到ReLU-GNN与{TrReLU,id}-GNN在布尔特征图上的表达能力对比。

Method: 作者通过在有限简单无向图（配备单一布尔节点特征）这一设定下，对Σ-MPLang（任意最终常数激活函数集合Σ，任意实数系数）与ReLU-MPLang的表达能力进行严格的包含关系证明，证明前者是后者的严格子类。

Result: 证明了Σ-MPLang可表达的布尔查询类是ReLU-MPLang可表达布尔查询类的严格子类，由此推论出ReLU-GNN在布尔查询方面严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN。

Conclusion: 该结果最终确认了ReLU-MPLang在布尔查询表达能力上严格超越trReLU-MPLang，即在布尔特征的图上，ReLU激活的GNN比TrReLU与恒等激活组合的GNN表达能力更强。

Abstract: We prove that, on finite simple undirected graphs equipped with a single Boolean node feature, the Boolean queries expressible in $Σ$-MPLang, for any collection $Σ$ of eventually constant activation functions and with arbitrary real coefficients, form a strict subclass of the Boolean queries expressible in ReLU-MPLang. We thereby settle a recently posed open problem: whether ReLU-MPLang is more powerful than trReLU-MPLang when it comes to Boolean queries. In particular, this implies that ReLU-GNNs are strictly more expressive than {TrReLU,id}-GNNs with respect to Boolean queries on Boolean-featured graphs.

</details>


### [127] [Structure-preserving uncertainty quantification for GENERIC dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12624)
*Zequn He,Celia Reina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构保持的认知神经网络（S-PENNs），一种面向具有硬结构约束的科学机器学习模型的不确定性量化通用框架，并通过轻量级epinets确保所有采样实现保持物理可行性，结合分裂共形预测提供有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法应用于硬约束结构保持模型时存在三大问题：可能违反编码的物理可容许条件、需要修改模型架构、或带来高昂计算成本。因此亟需一种既能保持物理结构又能高效量化不确定性的通用框架。

Method: S-PENNs通过在预训练模型的约束组件上附加轻量级epinets来保持结构约束，使每次采样都满足物理可容许性；针对GENERIC动力学实例化该框架以保持热力学一致性（第一和第二定律）；进一步结合分裂共形预测作为事后校准方法，产生具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在三个数值实例（耦合热浴的谐振子、理想化学马达（均基于ODE）、一维黏塑性模型（基于PDE））上验证，S-PENNs均产生热力学一致的随机实现和良好校准的预测区间，同时与深度集成相比计算成本降低约1到3个数量级。

Conclusion: S-PENNs作为一种通用框架，不仅适用于GENERIC动力学，还可扩展到计算力学中具有硬约束或软约束的广泛科学机器学习模型，为解决结构保持模型中的不确定性量化提供了高效且物理一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Structure-preserving machine learning embeds physical structure directly into model architectures, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such hard-constrained models remains limited because standard UQ methods may violate the encoded admissibility conditions, require architectural modifications, or impose substantial computational costs. In this work, we propose Structure-Preserving Epistemic Neural Networks (S-PENNs), a general framework for UQ in scientific machine learning models with hard architectural constraints, and instantiate it for GENERIC (General Equation for Non-Equilibrium Reversible-Irreversible Coupling) dynamics. S-PENNs preserve the structural constraints of a pretrained model by attaching lightweight epinets to its constrained components, ensuring that every sampled realization remains physically admissible by construction. When applied to GENERIC dynamics, such a proposed framework yields thermodynamically consistent rollouts that preserve the first and second laws. Furthermore, we combine S-PENNs with split conformal prediction as a post-hoc calibration method to produce prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees. We validate S-PENNs on three numerical examples: a harmonic oscillator coupled to a heat bath and an idealized chemical motor, both governed by ODEs, and a one-dimensional viscoplastic model governed by PDEs. Across all three examples, S-PENNs produce thermodynamically consistent stochastic realizations and well-calibrated prediction intervals while reducing the computational cost by about one to three orders of magnitude compared to deep ensembles. Although the present study focuses on GENERIC dynamics, S-PENNs can be extended more broadly to scientific machine learning models in computational mechanics with either hard or soft constraints.

</details>


### [128] [CAKE: Compiler-Agent Co-Design for Frontier Kernel Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12629)
*Zihao Ye,Yingyi Huang,Hongyi Jin,Bohan Hou,Junru Shao,Zhongming Yu,Jinqi Chen,Meghan Cowan,Shiyi Cao,Shanli Xing,Hanfeng Chen,Vinod Grover,Tianqi Chen,Luis Ceze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAKE是一个编译器-代理协同设计的系统，通过引入类型化、硬件显式的调度表示（CAKE IR），让GPU内核代理能够编写高性能内核代码。该IR暴露warp角色、内存移动、同步和流水线等关键调度决策，并支持验证、成本建模和局部诊断。在B200上，CAKE IR候选在Flash-KMeans测试中达到1.144x的FlashML基线性能（对比0.928x的CUDA/PTX），并在Kimi Delta Attention上实现2.05x的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核代理与GPU编程语言发展分离，导致专家级内核难以被代理复现。现有方案要么将编译器视为固定黑盒（代理仅收到错误、正确性结果和计时），要么通过难以使用的布局抽象隐藏关键调度决策。

Method: 提出CAKE编译器-代理协同设计框架：代理直接编写类型化、硬件显式的CAKE IR调度表示，暴露warp角色、内存移动、同步和流水线等硬件细节，同时提供验证、成本建模和局部诊断支持。系统本身具备自进化能力，重复失败可转化为验证规则、IR原语、模型校准和可复用优化策略。

Result: 在B200的Flash-KMeans测试中，CAKE IR候选（8000万token预算）达到1.144x的FlashML基线性能，显著优于直接CUDA/PTX的0.928x。Kimi Delta Attention实现2.05x几何平均加速并通过端到端serving验证；Dispatcher-backed KNN和KMeans在400+形状上提升1.42x至2.12x；4个内核变更已作为上游PR提交。

Conclusion: CAKE通过编译器-代理协同设计证明了将调度决策显式化于IR层面能够显著提升GPU内核代理的生成质量，并支持从单形状演化到库泛化与分派的完整流程。系统面向Ampere至Blackwell的NVIDIA GPU。

Abstract: GPU kernel agents and GPU programming languages have advanced separately, leaving expert kernels difficult to reproduce. Agents usually treat the compiler as a fixed black box and receive only errors, correctness outcomes, and timing, while existing DSLs either hide critical scheduling decisions or expose them through difficult layout abstractions. We present CAKE, a compiler-agent co-design in which agents author CAKE IR, a typed, hardware-explicit schedule representation. CAKE exposes warp roles, memory movement, synchronization, and pipelines while supporting verification, cost modeling, and localized diagnostics. The harness itself evolves: recurring failures become verifier rules, IR primitives, model calibrations, and reusable optimization tactics. In matched implementation-hidden Flash-KMeans clean starts on B200, the best CAKE IR candidate at an 80-million-token budget runs at 1.144x the tuned FlashML baseline, compared with 0.928x for direct CUDA/PTX. Beyond this benchmark, agent-generated Kimi Delta Attention achieves a 2.05x geometric-mean speedup over official FlashKDA and passes end-to-end serving validation. Dispatcher-backed KNN and KMeans improve performance by 1.42x to 2.12x across more than 400 shapes, and four kernel changes are available as upstream PRs. CAKE targets NVIDIA GPUs from Ampere through Blackwell and separates single-shape evolution from library generalization and dispatch.

</details>


### [129] [Interpretable Causal Discovery via Causal-Effect Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12640)
*Cixuan Zhang,Guy Van den Broeck,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件因果发现方法，将因果发现转化为贝叶斯推断问题，并利用稀有事件估计技术（粒子驱动方法）在联合图-参数空间中高效逼近后验分布，即使在目标事件后验质量很小的情况下也能有效工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的目标不仅是预测因果边，更要能解释观测到或假定的现象（如特别大的因果效应）。这种条件因果发现需要估计给定事件（如因果效应约束）下的后验分布，但现有贝叶斯因果发现方法在事件后验质量很小时计算上存在困难。

Method: 将条件因果发现建模为贝叶斯推断问题，目标是在给定事件（如因果效应约束）的条件下估计因果图和参数的后验。作者将稀有事件估计技术引入贝叶斯因果发现，通过逐步将粒子群向约束区域驱动，同时保持近似条件后验的样本，在联合图-参数空间中执行推断。

Result: 在合成图上的实证评估验证了该方法在小规模和大规模场景下的准确性，并在Sachs蛋白质数据集案例研究中展示了该方法如何通过提供通路级摘要来辅助科学探索。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决条件因果发现中的计算挑战，即使在事件后验质量很小的情况下也能准确估计条件后验，并能为科学探索提供可解释的通路级洞察。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover the underlying causal relationships given data generated from a system. The goal, however, is not merely to predict causal edges given data, but also to be able to interpret and explain either observed or hypothesized phenomena, such as a particularly large causal effect. We consider this task of conditional causal discovery and cast it as a Bayesian inference problem, in which we target the posterior over causal graphs and parameters conditional on an event such as a causal-effect constraint. Unfortunately, this poses a computational challenge: existing approaches to Bayesian causal discovery struggle when the event has small posterior mass. To address this, we adapt rare-event estimation techniques to perform inference the joint graph-parameter space. Our method gradually drives a particle population toward the constrained region while maintaining samples that approximate the conditional posterior. Empirical evaluation on synthetic graphs validates the accuracy of our approach at small and large scales, and we show in a case study on the Sachs protein dataset how our method can be used to aid scientific exploration by providing pathway-level summaries.

</details>


### [130] [Training Under Challenge: Executable Certificates and Challenge-Closed Optimality for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12655)
*Farhang Yeganegi,Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Training Under Challenge"框架，通过预声明的架构有效程序构造同类的备选模型并重新评估目标，提供可执行的证书来验证神经网络训练是否达到全局最优。在平方损失下，块下降算子使覆盖可检查并给出统一次残差界；在通道门控ResNet-18蒸馏问题上，八个内部挑战覆盖全部240个审计输出方向，实际残差界与真实差距的因子在1.74-3.02之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦的训练曲线无法区分网络是否达到全局最优、陷入局部陷阱、受表示能力限制或与训练器不匹配，需要一种可验证的框架来诊断训练状态并量化全局最优性差距。

Method: 提出可执行证书框架，通过预声明的架构有效程序构造同类备选模型并重新评估同一目标函数；任何更优候选作为可回放证据下界估计检查点的全局最优性差距。定义资源索引的挑战能力模量，用于刻画与通过兼容的最大差距；对平方损失使用块下降算子使覆盖可检查。

Result: 证明了无覆盖时一阶ReLU训练器可达到无穷多个精确条件头最优而收敛到非全局点。在通道门控ResNet-18蒸馏问题上，八个内部挑战覆盖所有240个审计输出方向，实测残差界与真实差距的因子为1.74-3.02。配对的预测证书区分解码器未充分利用与表示不足；量化去噪研究展示了诊断、修复和当前状态重新认证。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可操作、可回放的训练质量验证方法，能够区分不同类型的训练失败原因，并通过有限挑战套件和覆盖机制给出有意义的全局最优性差距下界。

Abstract: A flat training curve does not reveal whether a neural network has reached a global optimum, is locally trapped, is representation-limited, or is mismatched to its trainer. We introduce Training Under Challenge, an executable-certificate framework in which predeclared, architecture-valid procedures construct complete alternatives in the same certified class and reevaluate the same objective. Any lower-valued candidate is a replayable witness that lower-bounds the checkpoint's empirical global-optimality gap. Passing a finite suite is only suite-relative; global-gap conclusions require a separately justified coverage mechanism. We define a resource-indexed challenge-power modulus that characterizes the largest gap compatible with passage. For squared loss, current block-decrease operators make coverage checkable and yield uniform and realized-residual bounds. We prove the converse frontier: without coverage, a first-order ReLU trainer can reach infinitely many exact conditional head optima while converging to a non-global point. On a channel-gated ResNet-18 distillation problem with known optimum, eight internal challenges cover all 240 audited output directions, and realized-residual bounds lie within factors of 1.74--3.02 of the true gap. Paired predictive certificates separate decoder under-use from representation insufficiency, while quantized-denoising studies demonstrate diagnosis, repair, and current-state recertification.

</details>


### [131] [Demand Transfer Estimation at Scale via Restricted Logit Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12680)
*Lakshya Garg,Deep Narayan Mishra,Swapnil Yadav,Haoan Wang,Sujal Alugubelli,Karthik Kumaran,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在大规模商品集（超过100万商品）上高效估计需求转移（DT）系数的方法，在商品独立需求预测的基础上，通过DT系数调整来捕捉商品间替代关系，从而优化商品组合的需求预测，实验验证了该方法在满足一定替代行为假设时的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献主要聚焦于学习客户选择模型并用其评估商品组合的期望需求，但对于包含大量品类的大规模商品集，这种方法需要对每个可能的商品组合分别进行需求预测，效率低下。因此需要一种既保留独立需求预测高效性，又能考虑商品间替代关系的方法。

Method: 提出一种计算需求转移（DT）系数的方法。DT系数表示当目标商品（顾客进店想买的商品）被下架时，其需求被转移到商品集中其他各商品的比例。该方法能够在包含100万+商品的大规模商品集上高效计算这些系数。

Result: 在多个地点的历史交易数据及品类数据上的实验表明，当满足某些关于顾客替代行为的合理假设时，该方法能够准确估计潜在的DT系数，并带来需求预测的改进。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了独立需求预测的效率与替代关系的调整优势，为大规模商品集中的需求预测提供了可行的解决方案，在满足合理假设的条件下能有效提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Item demand forecasting is an integral component of store assortment optimization. Existing literature focuses on learning a suitable customer choice model and using this model to determine the value of an objective function (i.e. expected demand) with respect to an assortment proposal. However, for large item universe with many categories, this approach can prove inefficient, needing a separate demand forecast for every possible item assortment. An alternate approach exists whereby we combine the efficiency of forecasting item demand independently, while at the same time applying adjustments to the independent forecasts that account for the relations between item demand and the availability of other similar items on the shelf.
  Central to this approach is the estimation of Demand Transfer (DT) coefficients. These DT coefficients represent the percent of a particular target item's (item that the customer walked in the store to buy) demand that is redirected to each other item in the universe should the target item be removed from the shelf. We introduce an approach that allows us to compute these DT coefficients on large item universes (assortments having 1 million+ items). Experiments on data as well as historical transaction data for multiple locations within categories demonstrate that when certain reasonable assumptions about substitution behavior are satisfied, our procedure is able to accurately estimate underlying DT coefficients and lead to improvements in demand forecasting.

</details>


### [132] [Finding the Needle in a Haystack: Test-Time Analog Circuit Representation Adaptation for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12687)
*Fin Amin,Sounak Dutta,Paul D. Franzon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTARO是一种在线深度核贝叶斯优化框架，通过在整个搜索过程中持续适应电路表示来缩小表示学习与优化目标之间的差距。相比固定嵌入的BO和DKL，TTARO平均将遗憾AUC降低了15.2%和20.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于表示的贝叶斯优化方法在编码器训练后将电路嵌入视为固定不变，导致学习到的表示不一定按照优化目标（FoM）组织，造成表示学习与优化之间的不匹配问题。

Method: TTARO从预训练的电路嵌入出发，联合学习非线性特征变换和高斯过程代理模型，利用已评估电路的FoM标签。每次新评估后，更新表示和代理模型，再选择下一个候选点，从而使搜索空间与优化目标持续对齐。

Result: 在40种编码器/核/采集函数设置下，TTARO相对固定嵌入BO平均降低遗憾AUC 15.2%，相对DKL降低20.7%，多数设置下优于现有方法，最大降幅达46.7%。

Conclusion: 通过在搜索过程中持续将新观测到的FoM标签融入表示学习，TTARO有效实现了电路搜索空间与优化目标的对齐，展示了在线表示适应在模拟电路拓扑搜索中的显著价值。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a sample-efficient framework for analog circuit topology search, where evaluating each candidate topology can require costly simulation. However, representation-based BO methods typically treat circuit embeddings as fixed after encoder training. This creates a mismatch between representation learning and optimization: embeddings learned to encode or reconstruct circuit structure are not necessarily organized according to the figure of merit (FoM) being optimized. This paper introduces Test-Time Analog Representation Adaptation for Bayesian Optimization (TTARO), an online deep-kernel BO framework that adapts circuit representations throughout the search process. Starting from pretrained circuit embeddings, TTARO jointly learns a nonlinear feature transformation and a Gaussian-process surrogate using the FoM labels of the circuits evaluated so far. Following each new evaluation, TTARO updates the representation and surrogate before selecting the next candidate. We compare TTARO with conventional Gaussian Process-based BO over fixed embeddings and with Deep Kernel Learning (DKL), which learns the representation only from the initial evaluated designs and keeps it fixed throughout the remainder of the search. By continually incorporating newly observed FoM labels into representation learning, TTARO aligns the search space with the optimization objective as BO progresses. In our experiments, TTARO reduces regret AUC by 15.2% on average relative to BO and by 20.7% relative to DKL across 40 encoder/kernel/acquisition settings, outperforming prior art in most settings with reductions as large as 46.7%.

</details>


### [133] [The Impact of Temporal Context Length and Encoding Strategies on Self-Supervised ECG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12695)
*Ahmed Sameh,Ramzi Al-Sharawi,Yogatheesan Varatharajah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Icentia11k单导联心电数据集上进行了受控实验，系统研究了输入时间窗长度（16秒至10分钟）和前端表示方式（连续卷积补丁嵌入 vs 离散向量量化token）对自监督心电图模型性能的影响。结果表明，更长的输入时间窗（5-10分钟）能显著提升下游心律失常检测和患者级检索的准确性，且连续补丁嵌入在所有时间窗下均优于离散token表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督心电图模型通常仅使用几秒的心电信号进行训练，且越来越多的模型采用离散化token序列作为输入。然而，这些设计选择是否丢失了真实世界动态心电记录中用于节律推断和纵向一致性分析所需的信息，尚不明确。作者希望通过受控实验系统评估这些因素对模型性能的影响。

Method: 在Icentia11k单导联数据集上，在保持Transformer主干网络和训练协议一致的前提下，变化两个关键维度：(i)输入时间窗长度，包括16秒、1分钟、5分钟和10分钟四个水平；(ii)前端表示方式，包括连续卷积补丁嵌入和固定向量量化token两种。通过下游异常心律检测任务和患者级检索任务（探索跨会话稳定性）来评估各表示的性能。

Result: 实验结果显示：将时间上下文从16秒扩展至更长窗口能带来更强的迁移能力和更高的检索准确率，其中5分钟和10分钟模型表现最佳，表明它们更好地捕获了慢变化节律动力学和个体特异性结构。在所有评估的时间窗下，连续补丁嵌入均优于离散token表示，说明向量量化可能丢弃了临床相关的波形细节。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，心电图基础模型应当强调扩展的上下文信息和连续编码器，以更好地支持临床预测和基于相似性的应用。代码和预训练模型已公开。

Abstract: Self-supervised electrocardiogram (ECG) models are often trained on a few seconds of ECG signal and, increasingly, on discretized token sequences. It remains unclear whether these choices sacrifice information needed for rhythm inference and longitudinal consistency in real-world ambulatory recordings. We present a controlled study on the Icentia11k single-lead dataset that varies (i) the input horizon (16 seconds, 1 minute, 5 minutes, and 10 minutes) and (ii) the front-end representation (continuous convolutional patch embeddings vs. fixed vector-quantized tokens), while holding the Transformer backbone and training protocol constant. Representations are assessed by downstream abnormal rhythm detection and by patient-level retrieval that probes cross-session stability. Our results show that increasing temporal context beyond 16-second snapshots yields stronger transfer and higher retrieval accuracy, with the strongest performance achieved by the 5- and 10-minute models, indicating improved capture of slow-varying rhythm dynamics and individual-specific structure. Across all evaluated horizons, continuous patch embeddings outperform discretized tokens, suggesting that quantization can discard clinically relevant waveform detail. These findings motivate ECG foundation models that emphasize extended context and continuous encoders for clinical prediction and similarity-based applications. Our code and pretrained models are publicly available at https://github.com/muha-0/ecg-ssl-representation-learning.

</details>


### [134] [EEG Decoding Using CNN and LSTM Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13285)
*Athanasios Karagounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合卷积神经网络（CNN）和双向长短期记忆网络（bi-LSTM）的混合深度学习架构，用于运动想象脑电（MI-EEG）信号的解码。该架构利用CNN从原始MI-EEG记录中学习高层次的时空表征，再利用bi-LSTM建模特征间的时间依赖关系。在公开数据集和私有数据集上的实验结果表明，该架构在二分类和三分类运动想象任务中均表现出稳健的性能，并具有较好的受试者无关解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象脑机接口为中风或神经退行性疾病患者提供了灵活的脑-外设通信途径，但MI-EEG信号含有大量噪声且与大脑活动之间的关联复杂且信息较弱，导致可靠的解码仍然具有挑战性。虽然深度学习能够直接从EEG信号中学习表征，但其在MI-EEG特征学习方面的应用仍然较为有限。

Method: 提出一种混合深度学习架构，将CNN与bi-LSTM集成。CNN用于直接从原始MI-EEG记录中学习高层次的空间和时间表征，bi-LSTM则用于对提取特征之间的时间依赖关系和关联进行建模。

Result: 在公开数据集和自建数据集上的实验结果表明，CNN&bi-LSTM架构在二分类和三分类运动想象任务中均表现出稳健的性能，并在所评估的方法中展示了有前景的受试者无关解码能力。

Conclusion: 该CNN与bi-LSTM的混合架构为MI-EEG解码提供了一种有效的深度学习方法，能够同时捕获空间、时间特征及其时间依赖关系，在运动想象分类任务中具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) have emerged as a promising approach for establishing flexible communication pathways between the human brain and external devices , particularly for individuals affected by stroke or neurodegenerative disorders. Reliable decoding of motor-imagery electroencephalography (MI-EEG) remains challenging because EEG recordings contain substantial noise and exhibit complex, weakly informative relationships with the underlying brain activity. Although deep learning provides an effective means of learning representations directly from EEG signals, its application to MI-EEG feature learning remains comparatively limited. This study introduces a hybrid deep-learning architecture that integrates a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a bidirectional long short-term memory (bi-LSTM) network. The CNN is used to learn high-level spatial and temporal representations directly from raw MI-EEG recordings, whereas the bi-LSTM models temporal dependencies and relationships among the extracted features. The proposed approach is evaluated using both a publicly available dataset and a privately acquired dataset obtained with an EEG acquisition system. The experimental results indicate that the CNN\&bi-LSTM architecture provides robust performance for both two- and three-class motor-imagery classification and demonstrates promising subject-independent decoding capability across the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [135] [A Contract-Grade Verifier for LLM-Generated GPU Kernels, and a Native Blackwell Backward for the Gated-Linear-Recurrence Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12700)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有GPU内核生成系统的正确性验证过于宽松（仅用固定形状的少量随机输入测试），并构建了一个包含十二个对抗性检查的契约级验证器。用它对2,638个已被公共系统接受的机器生成内核进行审计，发现39.5%无法通过任何容差论证，62.1%至少有一项违规。作者还验证了自家GDN训练反向内核的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU内核生成的"高正确率"来自单一的宽松测试（固定形状、少量随机输入、宽松阈值），这掩盖了大量静默错误——如NaN/Inf被当作普通数字返回、结果不稳定、形状变化时出错、fp16累积误差等。需要更严格的验证机制来揭示真实正确性水平。

Method: 构建一个包含十二个对抗性门控的契约级验证器，每个门控是正确内核必须满足的属性，多个门控无容差（tolerance-free）。对外审计2,638个公共系统已接受的内核；对内用双精度oracle验证自研GDN训练反向内核，并用四种独立方式交叉验证（7/7阳性对照、阈值校准扫描、98.5%与参考基准一致、分层人工审计）。

Result: 审计发现39.5%的内核存在无法用容差解释的严重错误，62.1%至少有一项违规。现有标准测试接受而验证器拒绝的内核有1,487个，反向只有14个。自研的Blackwell tcgen05 GDN训练反向内核（含reverse-state阶段）通过独立验证，并成功训练五个家族成员。

Conclusion: 内核生成领域的正确性信号远弱于报告数字所暗示的，容差无关的契约验证可以弥合大部分差距。同时展示了如何在自家内核上使用这种严格验证建立独立正确性。

Abstract: Systems that generate GPU kernels with language models report high correctness rates. Those rates come from a single loose test: run the kernel on a few random inputs at one fixed shape and accept it if the output is close to a reference. A kernel can pass that test and still be silently wrong. It can return an ordinary number where the true answer is a NaN or an infinity, differ from run to run, break when the shape changes, or accumulate in fp16 where the reference keeps an fp32 total. We build the instrument that checks correctness properly: a contract-grade verifier of twelve adversarial gates, each a property a correct kernel must satisfy, several of them tolerance-free, so no choice of threshold can explain a failure away. Aimed outward, the verifier audits 2,638 machine-generated kernels that a public system's own harness had already accepted as correct. It finds 39.5% broken beyond any tolerance argument and 62.1% carrying at least one violation. The field's standard test accepts 1,487 kernels the verifier rejects, against only 14 the other way. We defend the finding four independent ways: a 7/7 positive control, a threshold-calibration sweep, 98.5% agreement with the reference benchmark's own correctness code, and a stratified hand-audit. Aimed inward, the verifier judges a kernel of our own: the first native Blackwell tcgen05 training backward for the gated-linear-recurrence (GDN) family, including the reverse-state stage the field still runs on a fallback. We establish its correctness independently, against a double-precision oracle, and train five family members through it. The correctness signal behind reported progress in kernel generation is far weaker than the numbers suggest, and a set of tolerance-free contracts would close most of the gap.

</details>


### [136] [Federated Compositional Muon Optimizer for Matrix-Wise Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12710)
*Wang Yan,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的联邦组合优化器FedCoMuon及其方差缩减变体FedCoMuon-VR，用于解决分布式矩阵级组合优化问题。通过结合组合梯度跟踪和正交化动量，FedCoMuon-VR在非独立同分布和非凸设定下达到了O(ε⁻³)的更优样本复杂度，并在鲁棒联邦学习和风险敏感元学习任务上取得了最佳精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器虽然在矩阵级AI模型中表现优异，但现有方法及其变体并不适用于分层结构的优化问题，特别是在联邦学习场景下的分布式矩阵级组合优化问题中缺乏有效方案。

Method: 提出FedCoMuon优化器，基于组合梯度跟踪和正交化动量技术构建；进一步引入基于动量的方差缩减技术，提出FedCoMuon-VR变体以降低方差并提升收敛性能。

Result: 在非独立同分布和非凸设定下完成收敛性分析，FedCoMuon-VR达到O(ε⁻³)的样本复杂度，优于现有FedMuon算法。数值实验在鲁棒联邦学习和任务分布式风险敏感元学习上展现了竞争力，并在多个设定下取得最佳精度。

Conclusion: 提出的FedCoMuon和FedCoMuon-VR有效填补了Muon优化器在分层结构联邦组合优化中的空白，为分布式矩阵级优化提供了更高效且具有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Muon, a more recently developed optimizer, is useful for matrix-wise models in AI areas. Although many works have studied Muon and its variants, these methods are still not particularly well-suited for hierarchical structured problems. To fill this gap, we propose an effective federated compositional Muon (FedCoMuon) optimizer to solve distributed matrix-wise compositional optimization problems. Specifically, our FedCoMuon optimizer builds on compositional gradient tracking and orthogonalized momentum. Moreover, we propose a variance reduced variant of FedCoMuon (FedCoMuon-VR) based on a momentum-based variance reduced technique. In theory, we analyze the convergence properties of our algorithms under the non-i.i.d. and non-convex settings. In particular, we prove that our FedCoMuon-VR obtains a lower sample complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary solution than the existing FedMuon algorithms. Extensive numerical experiments on robust federated learning and task-distributed risk-sensitive meta learning show that our proposed methods are competitive with existing compositional baselines and achieve the best reported accuracy in several settings.

</details>


### [137] [Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping (PRISM): naming-error dissociations in language models and post-stroke aphasia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12717)
*Xiang Guan,Roger D. Newman-Norlund,Yong Yang,Saeed Ahmadi,Regan Willis,Nadra Salman,Kalil Warren,Srihari Nelakuditi,Chris Rorden,Leonardo Bonilha,Julius Fridriksson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PRISM框架，将人类神经影像学中的减法分析（subtraction analysis）范式迁移到大型语言模型的机制可解释性研究中，将扰动种子视为"被试"，在层轴上做基于阈值的聚类增强分析。该框架与213名脑卒中后失语症患者的病灶-症状映射进行结构匹配的平行分析，发现LLM和大脑皮层都稳健地复现了音位优势的分离模式（深层聚类和额叶-外侧裂皮层聚类），为Transformer语言模型的功能特化主张提供了可证伪、空间分辨的检验工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的机制可解释性缺乏空间分辨、可证伪的工具来检验内部组件是否专司特定认知操作，作者试图将人类神经影像学成熟的减法分析框架迁移到扰动Transformer中。

Method: 基于Brain-LLM Unified Model（BLUM）框架，开发PRISM方法：对LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B做逐层扰动，将费城命名测试的七类错误类别两两相减，每个扰动种子作为一个"被试"进行组分析，在层轴方向使用阈值自由聚类增强（TFCE）。同时在213名慢性脑卒中失语症患者上执行相关性差异病灶-症状映射的结构匹配分析，两均在留出集上验证。

Result: LLM和皮层两个基质均稳健地复现出音位偏好分离：LLM中呈现深层聚类，皮层中呈现额叶-外侧裂聚类，且结果均可复现。语义偏好方向在两种基质上均为一致符号但未达显著性的趋势。

Conclusion: PRISM为Transformer语言模型提供了可证伪、空间分辨的功能特化检验工具，证明了LLM微扰与大脑损伤之间的结构平行性；但最强的因果机制主张需要通过确认性的ROI层面干预（PRISM第三阶段）来授权。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of large language models lacks spatially resolved, falsifiable tools for testing whether internal components are specialized for distinct cognitive operations. We adapt subtraction analysis, the standard framework of human neuroimaging, from biological brains to perturbed transformers, and apply the same logic to both substrates in parallel. Building on the Brain-LLM Unified Model (BLUM), which showed that layer-perturbed LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B error profiles match the lesion patterns of aphasic patients, we develop PRISM (Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping). PRISM maps the seven clinical Philadelphia Naming Test categories, subtracts error classes pairwise, and treats each perturbation seed as a subject in a group analysis with threshold-free cluster enhancement along the layer axis. We run a structurally matched analysis on 213 chronic post-stroke aphasia patients using correlation-difference lesion-symptom mapping, and replicate both sides on held-out splits. The designs match in subject dimension (seeds, patients), spatial dimension (layers, atlas-parcellated cortex) and thresholding, but the contrast operator differs: a within-subject error-proportion difference for the LLM, a between-subject correlation difference for the cortex. Both substrates recover a robust phonemic-favoring dissociation, a deep layer cluster and a frontal-perisylvian cortical cluster, both replicating; the semantic-favoring direction is a consistently signed but non-significant trend on both. PRISM thus gives a falsifiable, spatially resolved test of functional-specialization claims in transformer language models. A confirmatory ROI-level intervention (PRISM Stage 3) licensing the strongest causal-mechanism claim is left to subsequent work.

</details>


### [138] [MAG: MAnifold Guided Semi-Supervised Multi-modal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12724)
*Zirui Cheng,Xun Xu,Tiankai Chen,Fady Rezk,Bowen Zheng,Xiaodong Shi,Shijie Li,Kangkang Lu,Bharadwaj Veeravalli,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAG框架，利用无标签多模态数据通过流形引导的半监督传播机制来选择上下文演示（demonstrations），以提升少样本多模态上下文学习（ICL）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的少样本上下文学习性能高度依赖于所选演示样本的质量和覆盖度。虽然无标签多模态数据丰富易得，但如何有效利用它们来辅助ICL演示选择尚不明确。

Method: MAG将演示选择形式化为多模态图上的半监督传播问题，采用两阶段策略：(i) 相关性分数传播识别高影响力的无标签样本进行伪标注，以降低MLLM推理成本；(ii) 利用多模态相关性选择最终演示。研究发现文本表示更适合相关性传播，而视觉和文本模态对高质量演示选择都至关重要。

Result: 在八个多模态基准上的实验表明，MAG在标签稀缺场景下持续优于强基线，在有限的伪标注预算下取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MAG证明了无标签数据可以有效地辅助多模态ICL演示选择，通过半监督传播策略在降低推理成本的同时提升性能，且揭示了不同模态在传播与选择阶段的不同作用。

Abstract: Few-shot in-context learning (ICL) with multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) enables task adaptation without parameter updates, but its performance is highly sensitive to the quality and coverage of the selected demonstrations. While unlabeled multi-modal data is abundant, it remains elusive how to exploit them for ICL. We propose MAG (MAnifold-Guided semi-supervised in-context demonstra- tion selection), an efficient framework that leverages unlabeled data to improve multi-modal ICL. MAG formulates demonstration selection as a semi-supervised propagation problem on a multi-modal graph and adopts a two-stage strategy: (i) relevance score propagation identifies a compact set of high-impact unlabeled samples for pseudo-labeling, reducing MLLM inference cost; (ii) multi-modal relevance is used to select the final demonstrations. We show that textual represen- tations are more effective for relevance propagation, while both visual and textual modalities are crucial for high-quality demonstration selection. Experiments on eight multi-modal benchmarks demonstrate that MAG consistently outperforms strong baselines in label-scarce regimes, achieving significant gains with a limited pseudo-labeling budget.

</details>


### [139] [A Cloud-Edge System for Multimodal Clinical Screening in Resource-Constrained Rural Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12745)
*Hei Ting,Chan,Chenwei Wu,Xueshen Liu,Zesen Zhao,Boyuan Zheng,Luis Filipe Nakayama,Michael G. Morley,Liyue Shen,Jiasi Chen,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种云-端协同架构，通过边缘端轻量级领域模型将原始医疗数据转化为结构化输出，再由云端LLM整合生成临床摘要，以解决资源受限的农村医疗场景中带宽稀缺、计算有限和多模态整合困难的问题。在20个多模态临床案例和三种网络配置下，该方法实现了98-99%的诊断工具召回率和92-96%的精确率，保持带宽无关的延迟（25-35秒），并将token成本降低4-15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管医疗AI已达到专家级诊断精度，但在带宽稀疏、计算资源有限且需要整合异构多模态数据的农村环境中，这些能力难以落地应用。现有云中心化的方法受限于网络条件，无法在资源受限场景下有效工作。

Method: 采用云-端协作的混合架构：边缘端部署轻量级领域专用模型，将原始医疗数据转换为紧凑的结构化输出；云端LLM负责将这些输出综合为临床摘要。此外引入基于LLM的编排器，根据患者上下文动态选择诊断工具，避免处理无关输入，提升模态覆盖的全面性。

Result: 在涵盖心脏、产科、创伤和筛查场景的20个多模态临床案例上，模拟三种网络配置（500kbps-5Mbps）进行评估。混合系统在诊断工具召回率上达到98-99%，精确率92-96%，临床准确性与云端基线持平或更优，延迟保持带宽无关（25-35秒），token成本降低4-15倍。

Conclusion: 架构设计在实现多模态高效整合和改善事实基础方面发挥关键作用，相比纯云方法，在部署约束下能有效提升效率和准确性，为资源受限环境下的医疗AI应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical AI has demonstrated specialist-level diagnostic accuracy, yet these capabilities remain largely inaccessible in resource-constrained rural settings where bandwidth is scarce, compute is limited, and clinical decision-making requires integrating heterogeneous modalities. We introduce a cloud--edge collaborative architecture that addresses these constraints: lightweight, domain-specific models on the edge transform raw medical data into compact structured outputs, while a cloud LLM synthesizes these outputs into clinical summaries. An LLM-based orchestrator dynamically selects diagnostic tools based on patient context, promoting comprehensive modality coverage without processing irrelevant inputs. We evaluate on 20 multimodal clinical cases spanning cardiac, obstetric, trauma, and screening scenarios under three simulated network profiles (500,kbps--5,Mbps). The hybrid system achieves 98--99% diagnostic tool recall with 92--96% precision, matches or exceeds cloud-only baselines on clinical accuracy, and maintains bandwidth-invariant latency (25--35,s) at 4--15x lower token cost. These results highlight the role of architectural design in enabling efficient multimodal integration and improving factual grounding compared to cloud-only approaches under deployment constraints.

</details>


### [140] [Decentralized Multi-Player Q-Learning in Episodic Markov Decision Processes with Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12753)
*Larissa Xu,King Bi,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种信息不对称场景下的去中心化多智能体强化学习问题，提出mQ-learning、mQ-learning-intervals（用于A、B问题）和mEXC、mEXC-Bellman（用于C问题），分别达到与集中式联合动作基准同阶的后悔界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体强化学习中，智能体之间无法通信但需协作完成任务是常见且重要的场景。现有研究大多假设完整信息或者仅考虑单一信息不对称形式，缺乏对多种信息不对称场景下去中心化学习的系统研究，尤其是其与集中式学习性能差距的量化分析。

Method: 论文将问题分为三类：共同奖励但动作不可观测、动作可观测但奖励独立、动作不可观测且奖励独立。针对A、B两类问题提出基于Q学习的mQ-learning及mQ-learning-intervals算法；针对C类问题提出两阶段的探索-利用算法mEXC和mEXC-Bellman。

Result: A、B问题达到O~(√(H⁴S·A_joint·T))的后悔界；C问题达到O~(H(S·A_joint)^{1/3}·T^{2/3})的后悔界。两种后悔界与单智能体Q-learning（Jin et al., 2018）在集中式基准下同阶（至多差对数因子）。但注意A_joint随玩家数M呈指数增长，因此结果仅在小M或小动作集时最有意义。

Conclusion: 去中心化多智能体学习在多种信息不对称场景下可以达到与集中式学习可比的性能，表明智能体间无法通信的约束并不会带来regret阶上的根本退化，但动作空间的指数增长限制了该方法在大规模多智能体场景中的实用性。

Abstract: We study decentralized multi-player reinforcement learning in episodic tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs) under three forms of information asymmetry: (A) unobserved actions with common rewards, (B) observed actions with independent rewards, and (C) unobserved actions with independent rewards. Players cannot communicate during learning but may agree on a protocol a priori. For Problems A and B we propose \texttt{mQ-learning} and \texttt{mQ-learning-intervals}, achieving $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{H^4 S A_{\text{joint}}\, T})$ regret, where $H$ is the horizon, $S$ the state count, $T = KH$ the total steps, and $A_{\text{joint}} = \prod_{i=1}^M |\mathcal{A}_i|$ the joint action space across $M$ players. For Problem C we give \texttt{mEXC} and \texttt{mEXC-Bellman}, two-phase explore-then-commit algorithms with regret $\tilde{O}(H (S A_{\text{joint}})^{1/3} T^{2/3})$. Against the centralized joint-action benchmark, decentralized learning under information asymmetry matches the single-agent Q-learning rate of \cite{jin2018q} up to logarithmic factors. Because $A_{\text{joint}}$ grows exponentially in $M$, the bounds are most meaningful for small $M$ or small per-player action sets.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Outcome Rewards: Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12764)
*Haoze Wu,Chuqiao Kuang,Tianyi Zhuang,Xiaoguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSPO框架，通过证据锚点（Evidence Anchors）和步骤级自蒸馏策略优化解决深度搜索智能体中GRPO奖励稀疏和自蒸馏信息不对称的问题，在Qwen3-8B上以约5%的额外开销超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体轨迹跨度数十步，而标准强化学习每轨迹只提供一个结果奖励，过于稀疏。OPSD虽可用模型自身logits作为密集token级教师，但扩展到搜索智能体时，教师因拥有特权信息（如正确答案）产生的分布与学生探索式推理分布存在系统性差异，朴素蒸馏会让学生继承这种信息不对称而非学习更好的搜索策略。

Method: 两个核心贡献：(1) 构建证据锚点（Evidence Anchors），从网络中提取简洁的步骤级证据片段作为特权信息，捕获关键推理步骤但不泄露完整答案路径；(2) 提出步骤级自蒸馏策略优化（SSPO），将师生分歧转换为GRPO中的步骤级优势权重，仅应用于错误轨迹。结果奖励决定策略更新方向，教师调节每步更新幅度，正确轨迹保持不变以保留多样性。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，SSPO在BrowseComp、GAIA和FRAMES基准上持续优于GRPO，超越或匹配使用两倍梯度步数训练的GRPO，且每步仅增加约5%的额外开销（来自单次额外前向传播）。

Conclusion: SSPO有效地解耦了"更新什么"与"更新多少"：结果奖励决定方向，教师调节幅度，仅对错误轨迹进行步骤级指导。这一设计既解决了稀疏奖励问题，又避免了信息不对称的负面影响，以极小开销实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Deep search agents operate over trajectories spanning dozens of steps, yet standard reinforcement learning provides only a single outcome reward per trajectory, which is far too sparse for effective credit assignment. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) addresses this by using the model's own logits as dense token-level teachers, but extending it to search agents introduces a fundamental tension: the teacher, having access to privileged information such as the correct answer, produces a distribution that differs systematically from the student's exploration-based reasoning, and naive distillation causes the student to inherit this information asymmetry rather than learn better search strategies. We resolve this tension through two contributions. First, we construct Evidence Anchors, which are concise, step-level evidence snippets extracted from the web, as privileged information that captures key reasoning steps without revealing the entire answer path. Second, we propose Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization (SSPO), which converts teacher-student disagreement into step-level advantage weights within GRPO, applied exclusively to incorrect trajectories. This design decouples what to update from how much to update: the outcome reward determines the direction of policy change, while the teacher modulates its magnitude at each step. Correct trajectories are left untouched, preserving their diversity. On Qwen3-8B, SSPO consistently outperforms GRPO across BrowseComp, GAIA, and FRAMES, surpassing or matching GRPO trained with twice as many gradient steps while adding only about 5 percent overhead per step from a single additional forward pass.

</details>


### [142] [CoMedBench: A Multi-Source Benchmark of Synthetic Medical Data Fidelity and Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12805)
*Akanta Das,Al Amin Farhad,Mrinmoy Sarkar Anto,David Rehkopf,Ayin Vala,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMedBench基准，用于系统性评估合成医疗数据在机器学习模型开发中的可用性。基准涵盖37个数据集-任务对（20个静态表格+17个时序ICU任务），跨7个公共数据源，统一评估统计保真度和任务效用。结果显示：表格任务中合成数据保留90-97%的AUROC信号；时序ICU任务更具挑战性且对生成器敏感，AUROC保留率约82-95%，但AUPRC下降至64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录的直接使用受隐私法规、机构审查和数据使用协议限制，而合成数据作为替代方案有潜力，但以往研究通常只评估单一生成器、单一数据集或狭窄的下游任务，难以判断合成数据何时能支持模型开发、何时无法保留任务关键信号。需要一个可复现的统一基准来系统评估合成数据的价值。

Method: 构建CoMedBench可复现基准，在统一的临床有效性框架和共享的训练/评估引擎下评估多个生成器。覆盖两种模态（静态表格和时序数据）共37个数据集-任务对，源自7个公共数据源（MIMIC-III/IV、eICU、UCI、BRFSS、NHANES、pycox生存数据集）。通过比较在真实和合成数据上训练/测试的模型来评估统计保真度和任务效用。

Result: 表格任务上，参考生成器CoMed-CTGAN保留90.6%的平均AUROC效用，最强生成器CoMed-TVAE达97.3%。时序ICU任务更困难且对生成器敏感：CoMed-CTGAN保留81.6%的AUROC和仅64.0%的AUPRC，而CoMed-TVAE仍保留约95%的AUROC。

Conclusion: 合成数据在表格任务中能较好地保留下游任务信号，但在时序ICU任务中性能下降更显著且依赖生成器选择。合成数据可用作实际替代方案，但需要谨慎评估，尤其是在时序和类别不平衡场景下。

Abstract: Access to clinical data is essential for developing reliable healthcare machine learning systems, but direct use of electronic health records is constrained by privacy regulation, institutional review, data-use agreements, and the risk of re-identification. Synthetic data promises a practical alternative: it can preserve useful statistical and clinical structure while reducing exposure of sensitive patient records.
